@@ -541,6 +555,51 @@
     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// The wavefront is time-ordered, but all receivers at a given time are
+// independent.  Each MPI rank owns a contiguous receiver range; its GPU
+// computes that range and the completed rows are exchanged before the next
+// timestep.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                                size_t t, size_t first, size_t count,
+                                size_t source) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t sourceRad = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (sourceRad > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * sourceRad;
+    }
+    val_t emission = (i == source && t < timesteps / 2) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t first, size_t count) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; best = t; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = 0.5f * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

+static void mpiRowPartition(size_t n, size_t& first, size_t& count) {
+    first = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    count = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) - first;
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<val_t>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_FLOAT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<int>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_INT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

@@ -541,6 +555,51 @@
     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// The wavefront is time-ordered, but all receivers at a given time are
+// independent.  Each MPI rank owns a contiguous receiver range; its GPU
+// computes that range and the completed rows are exchanged before the next
+// timestep.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                                size_t t, size_t first, size_t count,
+                                size_t source) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t sourceRad = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (sourceRad > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * sourceRad;
+    }
+    val_t emission = (i == source && t < timesteps / 2) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t first, size_t count) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; best = t; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = 0.5f * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================
@@ -588,32 +647,76 @@

+static void mpiRowPartition(size_t n, size_t& first, size_t& count) {
+    first = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    count = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) - first;
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<val_t>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_FLOAT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<int>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_INT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// The wavefront is time-ordered, but all receivers at a given time are
+// independent.  Each MPI rank owns a contiguous receiver range; its GPU
+// computes that range and the completed rows are exchanged before the next
+// timestep.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                                size_t t, size_t first, size_t count,
+                                size_t source) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t sourceRad = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (sourceRad > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * sourceRad;
+    }
+    val_t emission = (i == source && t < timesteps / 2) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t first, size_t count) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; best = t; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = 0.5f * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

+static void mpiRowPartition(size_t n, size_t& first, size_t& count) {
+    first = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    count = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) - first;
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<val_t>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_FLOAT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<int>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_INT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// The wavefront is time-ordered, but all receivers at a given time are
+// independent.  Each MPI rank owns a contiguous receiver range; its GPU
+// computes that range and the completed rows are exchanged before the next
+// timestep.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                                size_t t, size_t first, size_t count,
+                                size_t source) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t sourceRad = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (sourceRad > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * sourceRad;
+    }
+    val_t emission = (i == source && t < timesteps / 2) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t first, size_t count) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; best = t; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = 0.5f * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================
@@ -588,32 +647,76 @@

+static void mpiRowPartition(size_t n, size_t& first, size_t& count) {
+    first = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    count = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) - first;
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<val_t>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_FLOAT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<int>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_INT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// The wavefront is time-ordered, but all receivers at a given time are
+// independent.  Each MPI rank owns a contiguous receiver range; its GPU
+// computes that range and the completed rows are exchanged before the next
+// timestep.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                                size_t t, size_t first, size_t count,
+                                size_t source) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t sourceRad = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (sourceRad > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * sourceRad;
+    }
+    val_t emission = (i == source && t < timesteps / 2) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t first, size_t count) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; best = t; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = 0.5f * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================
@@ -588,32 +647,76 @@

+static void mpiRowPartition(size_t n, size_t& first, size_t& count) {
+    first = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    count = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) - first;
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<val_t>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_FLOAT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<int>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_INT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }

+// The wavefront is time-ordered, but all receivers at a given time are
+// independent.  Each MPI rank owns a contiguous receiver range; its GPU
+// computes that range and the completed rows are exchanged before the next
+// timestep.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                                size_t t, size_t first, size_t count,
+                                size_t source) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t sourceRad = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (sourceRad > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * sourceRad;
+    }
+    val_t emission = (i == source && t < timesteps / 2) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t first, size_t count) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; best = t; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = 0.5f * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
 // ============================================================================

 // ============================================================================

+static void mpiRowPartition(size_t n, size_t& first, size_t& count) {
+    first = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    count = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) - first;
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<val_t>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_FLOAT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<int>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_INT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }

+// The wavefront is time-ordered, but all receivers at a given time are
+// independent.  Each MPI rank owns a contiguous receiver range; its GPU
+// computes that range and the completed rows are exchanged before the next
+// timestep.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                                size_t t, size_t first, size_t count,
+                                size_t source) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t sourceRad = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (sourceRad > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * sourceRad;
+    }
+    val_t emission = (i == source && t < timesteps / 2) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t first, size_t count) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; best = t; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = 0.5f * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

 // Precomputation Phase

+static void mpiRowPartition(size_t n, size_t& first, size_t& count) {
+    first = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    count = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) - first;
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<val_t>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_FLOAT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<int>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_INT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+

+            // OpenMP scheduling while retaining stochastic ray sampling.

+            RandomGenerator rng(seed);

-        }
     }

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }

+// The wavefront is time-ordered, but all receivers at a given time are
+// independent.  Each MPI rank owns a contiguous receiver range; its GPU
+// computes that range and the completed rows are exchanged before the next
+// timestep.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                                size_t t, size_t first, size_t count,
+                                size_t source) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t sourceRad = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (sourceRad > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * sourceRad;
+    }
+    val_t emission = (i == source && t < timesteps / 2) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t first, size_t count) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; best = t; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = 0.5f * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+

 // ============================================================================

 // Precomputation Phase
 // ============================================================================
 
+static void mpiRowPartition(size_t n, size_t& first, size_t& count) {
+    first = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    count = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) - first;
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<val_t>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_FLOAT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<int>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_INT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+
 void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
-    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
-    RandomGenerator rng(42);
-
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
+    if (g_mpiRank == 0) printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(state.numTriangles, first, count);
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+    for (size_t local = 0; local < count; ++local) {
+        size_t i = first + local;
         for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
             if (i == j) continue;
+            // A pair-local stream makes results independent of MPI rank and
+            // OpenMP scheduling while retaining stochastic ray sampling.

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }

+// The wavefront is time-ordered, but all receivers at a given time are
+// independent.  Each MPI rank owns a contiguous receiver range; its GPU
+// computes that range and the completed rows are exchanged before the next
+// timestep.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                                size_t t, size_t first, size_t count,
+                                size_t source) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t sourceRad = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (sourceRad > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * sourceRad;
+    }
+    val_t emission = (i == source && t < timesteps / 2) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t first, size_t count) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; best = t; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = 0.5f * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

 // Precomputation Phase
 // ============================================================================
 
+static void mpiRowPartition(size_t n, size_t& first, size_t& count) {
+    first = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    count = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) - first;
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<val_t>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_FLOAT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<int>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_INT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// The wavefront is time-ordered, but all receivers at a given time are
+// independent.  Each MPI rank owns a contiguous receiver range; its GPU
+// computes that range and the completed rows are exchanged before the next
+// timestep.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                                size_t t, size_t first, size_t count,
+                                size_t source) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t sourceRad = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (sourceRad > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * sourceRad;
+    }
+    val_t emission = (i == source && t < timesteps / 2) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t first, size_t count) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; best = t; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = 0.5f * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

 // Precomputation Phase

+static void mpiRowPartition(size_t n, size_t& first, size_t& count) {
+    first = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    count = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) - first;
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<val_t>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_FLOAT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<int>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_INT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// The wavefront is time-ordered, but all receivers at a given time are
+// independent.  Each MPI rank owns a contiguous receiver range; its GPU
+// computes that range and the completed rows are exchanged before the next
+// timestep.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                                size_t t, size_t first, size_t count,
+                                size_t source) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t sourceRad = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (sourceRad > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * sourceRad;
+    }
+    val_t emission = (i == source && t < timesteps / 2) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t first, size_t count) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; best = t; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = 0.5f * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

+static void mpiRowPartition(size_t n, size_t& first, size_t& count) {
+    first = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    count = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) - first;
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<val_t>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_FLOAT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<int>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_INT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }

+// The wavefront is time-ordered, but all receivers at a given time are
+// independent.  Each MPI rank owns a contiguous receiver range; its GPU
+// computes that range and the completed rows are exchanged before the next
+// timestep.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                                size_t t, size_t first, size_t count,
+                                size_t source) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t sourceRad = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (sourceRad > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * sourceRad;
+    }
+    val_t emission = (i == source && t < timesteps / 2) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t first, size_t count) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; best = t; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = 0.5f * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+

 // ============================================================================

+static void mpiRowPartition(size_t n, size_t& first, size_t& count) {
+    first = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    count = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) - first;
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<val_t>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_FLOAT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<int>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_INT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// The wavefront is time-ordered, but all receivers at a given time are
+// independent.  Each MPI rank owns a contiguous receiver range; its GPU
+// computes that range and the completed rows are exchanged before the next
+// timestep.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                                size_t t, size_t first, size_t count,
+                                size_t source) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t sourceRad = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (sourceRad > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * sourceRad;
+    }
+    val_t emission = (i == source && t < timesteps / 2) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t first, size_t count) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; best = t; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = 0.5f * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<int>& matrix, size_t n) {

+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
+    if (g_mpiRank == 0) printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(state.numTriangles, first, count);

+        size_t i = first + local;
         for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {

+            // A pair-local stream makes results independent of MPI rank and
+            // OpenMP scheduling while retaining stochastic ray sampling.
+            uint32_t seed = 42u ^ static_cast<uint32_t>(i * 0x9e3779b9u + j);
+            RandomGenerator rng(seed);

-        }

 }

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// The wavefront is time-ordered, but all receivers at a given time are
+// independent.  Each MPI rank owns a contiguous receiver range; its GPU
+// computes that range and the completed rows are exchanged before the next
+// timestep.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                                size_t t, size_t first, size_t count,
+                                size_t source) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t sourceRad = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (sourceRad > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * sourceRad;
+    }
+    val_t emission = (i == source && t < timesteps / 2) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t first, size_t count) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; best = t; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = 0.5f * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
 // ============================================================================

 // ============================================================================

+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);

+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<int>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);

+                   MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
+    if (g_mpiRank == 0) printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(state.numTriangles, first, count);
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+    for (size_t local = 0; local < count; ++local) {
+        size_t i = first + local;
         for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
             if (i == j) continue;
+            // A pair-local stream makes results independent of MPI rank and
+            // OpenMP scheduling while retaining stochastic ray sampling.
+            uint32_t seed = 42u ^ static_cast<uint32_t>(i * 0x9e3779b9u + j);
+            RandomGenerator rng(seed);
             state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(
                 i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
         }
-        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {
-            printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", i + 1, state.numTriangles);
-        }
     }
+    allGatherRows(state.kij, state.numTriangles);
 }

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// The wavefront is time-ordered, but all receivers at a given time are
+// independent.  Each MPI rank owns a contiguous receiver range; its GPU
+// computes that range and the completed rows are exchanged before the next
+// timestep.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                                size_t t, size_t first, size_t count,
+                                size_t source) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t sourceRad = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (sourceRad > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * sourceRad;
+    }
+    val_t emission = (i == source && t < timesteps / 2) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t first, size_t count) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; best = t; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = 0.5f * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

+    count = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) - first;

+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<val_t>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);

+}

+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<int>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_INT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+
+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
+    if (g_mpiRank == 0) printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(state.numTriangles, first, count);
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+    for (size_t local = 0; local < count; ++local) {
+        size_t i = first + local;
         for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
             if (i == j) continue;
+            // A pair-local stream makes results independent of MPI rank and
+            // OpenMP scheduling while retaining stochastic ray sampling.
+            uint32_t seed = 42u ^ static_cast<uint32_t>(i * 0x9e3779b9u + j);
+            RandomGenerator rng(seed);
             state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(
                 i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
         }
-        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {
-            printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", i + 1, state.numTriangles);
-        }
     }
+    allGatherRows(state.kij, state.numTriangles);
 }

codex
Implemented the hybrid accelerator-cluster parallelization.

Changes:

- MPI-distributed form-factor and time-delay matrix rows.
- OpenMP parallelism within each MPI rank.
- CUDA kernels for wave propagation and distance cross-correlation.
- MPI synchronization of timestep rows and final distances.
- CUDA device assignment per MPI rank.
- Updated CMake to require MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA while preserving executable name `roomsim`.
- No new source files or external dependencies.

Validation completed successfully with:

- CUDA build via CMake.
- Single-rank execution.
- Two-rank MPI execution.
- Runtime validation passing.

Generated build artifacts were removed.

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// The wavefront is time-ordered, but all receivers at a given time are
+// independent.  Each MPI rank owns a contiguous receiver range; its GPU
+// computes that range and the completed rows are exchanged before the next
+// timestep.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                val_t* radB, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                                size_t t, size_t first, size_t count,
+                                size_t source) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        val_t sourceRad = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (sourceRad > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * sourceRad;
+    }
+    val_t emission = (i == source && t < timesteps / 2) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source,
+                               size_t first, size_t count) {
+    size_t local = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= count) return;
+    size_t i = first + local;
+    val_t maxCorr = 0.0f;
+    size_t best = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; best = t; }
+    }
+    distances[i] = 0.5f * static_cast<val_t>(best);
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

+static void mpiRowPartition(size_t n, size_t& first, size_t& count) {
+    first = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+    count = n * static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiRank + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize) - first;
+}

+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<val_t>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_FLOAT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+
+static void allGatherRows(std::vector<int>& matrix, size_t n) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(g_mpiSize), displacements(g_mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < g_mpiSize; ++r) {
+        size_t first = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        size_t last = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(g_mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((last - first) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(first * n);
+    }
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(n, first, count);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, static_cast<int>(count * n), MPI_INT,
+                   matrix.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                   MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
+    if (g_mpiRank == 0) printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+    size_t first, count;
+    mpiRowPartition(state.numTriangles, first, count);
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+    for (size_t local = 0; local < count; ++local) {
+        size_t i = first + local;
         for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
             if (i == j) continue;
+            // A pair-local stream makes results independent of MPI rank and
+            // OpenMP scheduling while retaining stochastic ray sampling.
+            uint32_t seed = 42u ^ static_cast<uint32_t>(i * 0x9e3779b9u + j);
+            RandomGenerator rng(seed);

+    allGatherRows(state.kij, state.numTriangles);
